Kindle Rewards Terms of Use

Last Updated: November 7, 2025

These Terms of Use (“Kindle Rewards Terms”) are an agreement between you and Amazon.com Services LLC and/or its affiliates (“Amazon”, “we,” or “our”), and apply to your access to, and participation in, Kindle Rewards (“Kindle Rewards”), an Amazon service that allows customers to receive points on eligible book purchases through the Kindle Store located at 0 (“Kindle Points”), and to redeem those Kindle Points for Kindle book savings (“Rewards”). Kindle Rewards is an Amazon Service, and these Kindle Rewards Terms are Service Terms, each as defined in the Amazon.com Conditions of Use. By accessing or participating in Kindle Rewards, you agree to be bound by these Kindle Rewards Terms, the Amazon.com Conditions of Use, and all other applicable rules, policies, and terms posted on the 1 website (collectively, this “Agreement”).

Kindle Points

  • Eligible customers will receive 3 Kindle Points per $1.00 USD spent on eligible Kindle books and 1 Kindle Point per $1.00 USD spent on eligible print books, up to a maximum of 200 Kindle Points per book, or 400 Kindle Points per book on double and triple points days. Amazon reserves the right to determine which books are eligible to earn Kindle Points.
  • Customers may earn additional Kindle Points through bonus offers. All bonus offers will commence at 12:00 AM PST and end at 11:59 PM PST on the date(s) specified in the offer description on 0. Bonus offers vary.
  • Any Kindle Points earned for the purchase of a book that is subsequently returned or refunded will be deducted from your Kindle Points balance.
  • It may take up to 48 hours or more from the time Kindle Points are earned for the points to be reflected in your Kindle Points balance and available for use. You can view your Kindle Points balance and available bonus offers at 0.

Points Redemption

  • You may redeem 300 Kindle Points for $3.00 USD in savings towards your next eligible purchase of a Kindle book available through Amazon.com wherever points redemption is enabled. Kindle Points cannot be applied to the cost of any other items, including physical goods, Amazon Gift Cards, or digital subscriptions (such as Kindle Unlimited, Audible, and others).
  • Kindle Points must be redeemed within 3 months of the date of issuance, or before 11:59 PM PST on the date the Kindle Rewards program ends, whichever is earlier. Any Kindle Points not redeemed before this time will expire.
  • User experience and features for points redemption may vary depending on the device or application being used to access the Kindle Store.

Additional Terms

  • Kindle Rewards is currently only available to 0 customers located in the United States. Amazon reserves the right to determine customer eligibility for Kindle Rewards.
  • Taxes may apply to the full value of discounted and free promotional items.
  • Kindle Points, Rewards, and bonus offers have no cash value, are non-transferrable, and are non-refundable.
  • If Kindle Points are applied towards the purchase of an eligible book under $3.00 USD (including tax), then the maximum value of savings on that purchase will equal to the cost of the book plus tax.
  • You may receive a notification from Amazon regarding fulfillment of your Kindle Rewards offer.
  • If you subscribe to the Kindle Rewards email list, you are agreeing to opt-in to receive promotional and marketing emails about Kindle Rewards offers. You can unsubscribe from the Kindle Rewards emails at any time by visiting Your Email Subscriptions in Your Account.
  • Amazon reserves the right to modify, suspend, or discontinue Kindle Rewards, in whole or in part, including canceling or modifying any bonus offers, Kindle Points, or Rewards, at any time without notice, and the right to deny, limit, or cancel your use of Kindle Rewards at any time without notice, except as prohibited by law.
  • While we endeavor to give notice to changes to these Kindle Rewards Terms or this Agreement, we reserve the right to change these Kindle Rewards Terms, this Agreement, or any other aspect of Kindle Rewards without notice to you. If any such change is found invalid, void, or for any reason unenforceable, that change is severable and does not affect the validity and enforceability of any remaining changes or conditions. Your continued participation in Kindle Rewards after changes to the Kindle Rewards Terms or the Agreement constitutes your acceptance of the changes.

Kindle Support for Fire Tablets Released in 2012 or Earlier

Effective May 20, 2026, Amazon will end Kindle support for Fire Tablets released in 2012 or earlier.

The following Fire Tablets are impacted:

  • Kindle Fire 1st Generation
  • Kindle Fire 2nd Generation
  • Kindle Fire HD 7 2nd Gen
  • Kindle Fire HD 8.9 2nd Gen

Not sure which device you have? Identify your Fire Tablet.

After May 20, 2026, you will not be able to purchase, borrow, or download new books through the Kindle app.

What stays the same?

  • You can continue to access content that is already downloaded on your device.
  • Your Amazon account and Kindle library remain fully accessible on other devices and apps.
  • All other Amazon Services on the device won’t be impacted.

You have several options to continue accessing your entire Kindle library:

  • Free Kindle App: Available for Android, iOS, Mac, and PC at 0.
  • Kindle for Web: Read on your browser at Kindle Cloud Reader.
  • A new Kindle device or compatible Fire tablet: Your Kindle library is accessible on any Kindle E-Reader released after 2012, or on a compatible Amazon Fire tablet that supports the Kindle app.

kindle_reading_apps_setup_install_mobile

How do I install/update the Kindle Reading app for my mobile? {Keywords: register, android, iOS, iPad, iPhone, set up, register, login, log in} [kindle_reading_apps_setup_install_mobile]

Hello,

To install or update the mobile Kindle App:

  1. Make sure that your device meets the minimum requirements:
    • Mobile Devices:
      • iPhone with OS 16
      • Android phones with OS 10.0 or higher
    • Mobile Browsers:
      • Chrome 87f
      • Firefox 78f
      • Safari 11f
      • Opera 61f
      • UC Browser 10.0f
      • Samsung Browser 12.0f
  2. Go to your preferred app store on Android or the App Store on iOS.
  3. Search “Kindle”.
  4. From the search results, select “Amazon Kindle”.
  5. Select “Get” or “Install” to download the app.
  6. When the installation or update is complete, you may be presented with the terms of use. Please read and accept the terms of use.

I hope this information helps.

Learn About Kindle Reading Challenges

Reading challenges offer fun ways to celebrate and track your progress and help you build strong reading habits.

Kindle books purchased or borrowed from Amazon and read on your Kindle device, app, or Kindle for Web count towards books read, streaks, and achievements.

By linking your Amazon and Goodreads accounts, you can also collect Kindle achievements with eligible books from your Goodreads Read Shelf.

Keep watch for Mystery achievements, where you only discover the challenge as you progress. Challenge eligibility may vary by account. Some of the achievements are specific to you.

Note: To track your reading and access Reading Challenges in the Kindle app, read on your Kindle device or app with mobile data enabled.

You can share your challenge progress on social media using #ReadingChallenges #Kindle and join our Facebook group to connect with fellow readers. Request to join at facebook.com/groups/kindlereadingchallenge or type “Kindle Challenge” in the Facebook search bar.

  • How Do I Link or Unlink My Goodreads and Amazon Accounts?

Learn About Kindle Rewards

Kindle Rewards is a free program available for U.S. customers.

With Kindle Rewards, customers in the U.S. can earn Kindle Points when buying books or completing bonus offers. As a U.S. customer, you can then redeem your points for Kindle book savings. Kindle Rewards is a free program and you don’t need a Kindle Unlimited subscription to participate.

Earning Kindle Points:

  • You earn three points per dollar spent on Kindle books purchased on Amazon.com.
  • You earn one point per dollar spent on print books (excluding textbooks) purchased on Amazon.com.
  • When you buy an eligible Kindle book, your points appear as “pending” in your account. Pending points are Kindle Points you’ve earned but they aren’t available to use just yet. Most pending points become available to use within a few hours, but it may take up to 48 hours or more.

Using your Kindle Points:

  • Once you earn 300 Kindle Points, you can redeem your points on an eligible Kindle book’s detail page on the Amazon.com website.
  • You can redeem up to 300 points on the Amazon.com website during checkout. Please note, you do need to use USD (US $) to spend your Kindle Points. Points can only be redeemed when paying in USD (they cannot be used when using Amazon Currency Converter). To redeem points during checkout:
    1. Search for the Kindle Rewards icon near the Buy Now with 1-Click button.
    2. Select the checkbox to apply points to save on your Kindle book order.
  • Make sure that you redeem your points within three months of the issue date or they expire. You can review points expiring within the next 30 days in your Kindle Points balance.

Tip: You can only use Kindle rewards points to purchase digital books.

Visit Kindle Rewards to check your points balance and learn more.

Learn More About Ads On Kindle and Fire Tablet

If your device includes ads, sponsored screensavers appear on your device’s lock and home screens.

Amazon is able to offer some device models at a subsidized price by including personalized advertising on the device.

Kindle e-reader display ads as a screensaver when your device is in sleep mode and on your home screen marked as “Sponsored.” On Fire tablet, ads display on the lock screen. Sponsored content tiles in the Continue and Discover row on the Fire tablet home screen are an addition to our featured content. Tap on an advertisement for more details. Connect your device to the internet to receive the latest offers.

If you originally purchased a device with ads, you can unsubscribe by making a one-time payment to cover the difference between the device price with and without lockscreen ads. You can find more details on how to unsubscribe from lockscreen ads on our Help page: Remove Ads from Your Lockscreen.

If you have an eligible device and aren’t already subscribed to personalized ads, you can subscribe at no charge from the Manage Your Devices page.

Merge Your Amazon and Comixology Accounts

Read all your Comixology books or your Comixology Unlimited borrows on your Kindle e-reader or Kindle app by merging your Comixology and Amazon accounts.

Note:

If you’ve only ever used an Amazon login to access Comixology, you don’t need to merge any accounts. If you used an older Comixology username in the past, you can connect it with your Amazon login at Comixology.com/merge.

To connect your Comixology and Amazon accounts:

  1. Go to: Comixology settings.
  2. Select Connect your account.

Once merged, visit Read Comixology Books on Kindle.

If you merged the wrong Comixology and Amazon accounts, for support.

Be sure to include all relevant information such as the account email addresses and share a few purchased book titles for each account.

Read Comixology Books on Kindle

You can find the books that you bought on Comixology on Kindle apps as well as on Kindle for Web.

To have your Comixology purchases appear on Kindle:

  1. Select Show your Comixology books on your Comixology settings page.
  2. Open your Kindle app and swipe down to refresh your library. Depending on your Comixology library size it may take several hours for all of your comic books to appear in your Kindle library. Alternatively, you can select More, and then select Sync to refresh your library.

Note: Your Comixology comics, graphic novels, and manga won’t appear by default on your e-reader devices.

To read Comixology books on Kindle e-Reader devices:

  1. Go to your Content and Devices page.
  2. Select the book you want to read, and then Deliver the book to your registered Devices.

Due to memory limitations, we recommend you send fewer than 25 Comixology books to your e-reader device at a time. We also recommend you remove the book from your device when you have finished the book.

Kindle for Web is a web-based version of Kindle where you can read your books on your mobile or desktop web browser.

To read Comixology Books on Kindle for Web:

  1. Select your marketplace:
    • United States
    • United Kingdom
  2. Open the book you want to read.

Tip: You can hide your Comixology purchases on Kindle at any time selecting Hide your Comixology books on your Comixology settings page.

Safety & Compliance Information for Kindle (11th Generation)

FOR SAFETY AND COMPLIANCE INFORMATION FOR THE KINDLE KIDS DEVICE, PLEASE SCROLL DOWN TO “SAFETY AND COMPLIANCE INFORMATION - FOR KINDLE KIDS DEVICE”

Safety and Compliance Information - FOR KINDLEUse Responsibly. Read all instructions and safety information before use.

Screenshot: exclamation alert icon

FAILURE TO FOLLOW THESE SAFETY INSTRUCTIONS COULD RESULT IN FIRE, ELECTRIC SHOCK, OR OTHER INJURY OR DAMAGE.

Screenshot: hearing alert icon

AVOID PROLONGED LISTENING AT HIGH VOLUME. Prolonged listening of the player at high volume can damage the ear of the user. To prevent possible hearing damage, users should not listen at high volume levels for long periods. Audio is not available in all regions.

Maintaining Your Kindle DeviceGeneral Care

Clean the screen with a soft cloth; be careful not to wipe it with anything abrasive. When carrying your Kindle in a bag or briefcase, keep a cover on it to avoid scratches.

Don’t expose your Kindle to extreme heat or cold. For example, don’t leave it in the trunk of your car in sub-zero or high-heat conditions.

Use of third-party accessories may affect your Kindle device’s performance. In limited circumstances, use of third-party accessories may void your Kindle device’s limited warranty. Read all safety instructions for any accessories before using them with your Kindle.

Servicing Your Device

If your device needs service, please contact Amazon Customer Support. Contact details can be found at 0.

Faulty service may void the warranty.

Protection Against Water

Your device is not intended for underwater use and may experience temporary effects from exposure to water. If your device is immersed in water, follow these instructions:

  • Quickly retrieve your device. If your device has a cover, remove the cover.
  • If your device was immersed in fresh water, unplug all cables and drain your device by letting it stand upright with the USB port facing down.
  • If your device was immersed in other types of water, unplug all cables, drain your device by letting it stand upright with the USB port facing down, rinse your device off using fresh water (such as cold tap water) with the USB port facing the water source, and drain your device again.
  • Let the device dry fully in a well-ventilated location before plugging the USB cable into your device.
  • Do not attempt to dry your device with an external heat source (such as a microwave oven or hair dryer).
  • Do not insert a foreign object into the USB port (such as a cotton bud or paper towel).

To reduce the risk of damage to your device, follow these instructions:

  • Do not intentionally immerse your device in water or expose it to seawater, salt water, chlorinated water or other liquids (such as beverages).
  • Do not spill any food, oil, lotion or abrasive substances on your device.
  • Do not expose your device to pressurised water, high-velocity water or extremely humid conditions (such as a steam room).

If your device is dropped or otherwise damaged, the waterproofing of the device may be compromised.

For more information concerning care instructions and the waterproofing of your device, please see 0.

Battery SafetyScreenshot: exclamation alert icon

HANDLE WITH CARE. Your device contains a rechargeable lithium-ion polymer battery and should only be replaced by a qualified service provider. Do not disassemble, open, crush, bend, deform, puncture, shred or attempt to access the battery in your device. Do not modify or remanufacture the battery, attempt to insert foreign objects into the battery, or immerse or expose it to water or other liquids, expose to fire, explosion or other hazard. Only use the battery for the system for which it is specified. Use of an unqualified battery or charger may present a risk of fire, explosion, leakage or other hazard. Do not short-circuit a battery or allow metallic conductive objects to come into contact with battery terminals. Avoid dropping the device or battery. If the device or battery is dropped, especially on a hard surface, and the user suspects damage, do not attempt repair. Contact Amazon Customer Service for assistance.

Keep the device and its power adapter (sold separately) in a well-ventilated area and away from heat sources, especially when in use or charging. For more information about batteries, go to 0. Your device uses the USB cable provided and a USB charger or USB port that is compliant with safety standards, such as IEC 62368. Do not charge your device near water or in extremely humid conditions.

It is normal for your device and charger to become warm while charging. If your device becomes uncomfortably hot while charging, remove the device from the charger and wait until it cools down before resuming charging. If your device feels too warm to use after charging, wait until it cools down.

Other Safety Considerations

  • Engaging in repetitive motions, such as pressing keys or playing some games, may cause you to experience occasional discomfort in your hands, arms, shoulders, neck or other parts of your body. Discontinue use of your device and consult a physician if you experience headaches, blackouts, seizures, convulsion, eye or muscle twitching, loss of awareness, involuntary movement, disorientation or other discomfort. To reduce risk of these symptoms, avoid prolonged use, hold your device some distance from your eyes, use your device in a well-lit room and take frequent breaks.
  • Small parts contained in your device and its accessories may present a choking hazard to small children.
  • Using your Kindle while operating a vehicle is not recommended and may be prohibited or restricted in some areas. If you are in an area where using your Kindle while operating a vehicle is allowed, always give full attention to the operation of the vehicle and stop using your Kindle if it becomes a distraction.
  • USE CAUTION AROUND PACEMAKERS AND SIMILAR MEDICAL DEVICES. Personal medical devices, such as pacemakers, may be sensitive to magnetic and electromagnetic ﬁelds. As the device and certain accessories contain magnets and emit electromagnetic ﬁelds, they should be kept at least 15 cm (approximately 6 inches) from any personal medical device. If any interference is observed, consult with your doctor before resuming use. Items with magnetically stored data, such as credit cards and hard drives, may also be sensitive to magnetic or electromagnetic ﬁelds and should not be placed near these products.

Wireless Safety and Compliance

Turn your wireless connection off in areas where wireless use is forbidden or when it may cause interference or danger. Some specific situations are described below. In general, you should not use your Kindle with your wireless connection turned on any place you are not allowed to use a cellular device.

Turn Off Wireless When Flying

To prevent possible interference with aircraft systems, the US Federal Aviation Administration regulations and many other aviation agencies require you to have permission from a crew member to use your device’s wireless service.

Using Your Kindle Around Other Electronic Devices

Kindle generates, uses and can radiate radio frequency (RF) energy and, if not used in accordance with its instructions, may cause interference to radio communications and electronic equipment. External RF signals may affect improperly installed or inadequately shielded electronic operating systems, entertainment systems and personal medical devices. While most modern electronic equipment is shielded from external RF signals, if in doubt, check with the manufacturer. For personal medical devices (such as pacemakers and hearing aids), consult your physician or the manufacturer to determine whether they are adequately shielded from external RF signals.

Steps to Minimize Interference

If your Kindle does cause interference to radio or television reception (which you can determine by turning your Kindle off and on), you can try to correct the interference using one or more of the following measures: reorient or relocate the receiving antenna for the radio or television, increase the separation between the radio or television and your Kindle, connect equipment and receivers to different outlets, or consult the radio or television manufacturer or an experienced radio/TV technician for help.

Watch for Signs

There are some places where RF signals could constitute a hazard, such as health care facilities and construction sites. If you are not sure, look around for signs indicating that two-way radios or cell phones should be turned off.

In addition, turn off and do not charge your device if you are in any area with a potentially explosive atmosphere, as sparks in such areas could cause an explosion or fire. Such areas are usually, but not always, clearly identified, and include areas where you would normally be advised to turn off your vehicle engine.

FOR CUSTOMERS IN THE UNITED STATESFCC Compliance Statement

This device and its related accessories like the adapter (the “Products”) comply with part 15 of the FCC Rules. Operation of each Product is subject to the following two conditions: (1) each Product may not cause harmful interference, and (2) each Product must accept any interference received, including interference that may cause undesired operation.

Note: The Products have been tested and found to comply with the limits for a Class B digital device or external switching power supply, pursuant to part 15 of the FCC Rules. These limits are designed to provide reasonable protection against harmful interference in a residential installation. The Products generate, use and can radiate radio frequency energy and, if not installed and used in accordance with the instructions, may cause harmful interference to radio communications. However, there is no guarantee that interference will not occur in a particular installation. If a Product does cause harmful interference to radio or television reception, which can be determined by turning the Product off and on, the user is encouraged to try to correct the interference by one or more of the following measures:

  • Reorient or relocate the receiving antenna.
  • Increase the separation between the equipment and receiver.
  • Connect the equipment into an outlet on a circuit different from that to which the receiver is connected.

{47 CFR 15.21}

Pursuant to Section 15.21 of the FCC rules, changes or modifications to a Product by the user that are not expressly approved by the party responsible for compliance could void the user’s authority to operate the equipment.

The device meets the FCC Radio Frequency Emission Guidelines. Information on the product is on file with the FCC and can be found by inputting such Product’s FCC ID (which can be found on the device) into the FCC ID Search form available at 0.

The party responsible for FCC compliance is Amazon.com Services LLC 410 Terry Ave North, Seattle, WA 98109 USA. If you wish to contact Amazon visit 0, select United States, click Help & Troubleshooting, then click on Contact Us.

FRENCH SAR COMPLIANCE

Modèle RS23CV DAS tronc: 1.114 W/kg.

The local specific absorption rate (SAR) quantifies the user’s exposure to the electromagnetic waves of the equipment concerned. The maximum permitted SAR is 2 W/kg for the head and trunk and 4 W/kg for the limbs.

Reducing Exposure to Radiation from Radio Equipment.

To reduce radiation exposure from the device, use it in a location where you have strong reception. You can take steps to decrease children’s exposure to radiation from the device by, among other things, limiting the frequency and duration of their use of the device and reducing use at night. Keep the device away from the belly of pregnant women and the lower abdomen of adolescents.

WARNING: CHOKING HAZARD- Small parts. Not for children under 6 years of age

CAUTION - ELECTRICALLY OPERATED PRODUCT. Not recommended for children under 6 years of age. As with all electric products, precautions should be observed during handling and use to prevent electric shock.

The device and its included accessories are not toys. Children should be supervised to ensure proper use. Parents should also routinely check to confirm that the Device shows no signs of damage or breakage and is in a safe condition for use by children. The device is not suitable for children under age 6.

ALWAYS INSPECT FOR BREAKAGE AND USE WITH A CHILD-FRIENDLY COVER. If the device is dropped or impacted, parents should remove the child-friendly cover and inspect the device and cover for breaks, chips or cracks before putting the cover back on the device and permitting a child to resume use.

Screenshot: hearing alert icon

AVOID PROLONGED LISTENING AT HIGH VOLUME. Listening to the Device for prolonged periods at high volume can damage the ears of the user. To prevent possible hearing damage, users should not listen at high volume levels for long periods. Audio is not available in all regions.

Do not expose your Device or adaptor to liquids. Do not use your Device near sinks or other wet locations. Take care not to spill any food or liquid on your Device and do not put any object filled with a liquid, such as a vase, on or near your Device. If your Device or adaptor gets wet, carefully unplug all cables without getting your hands wet and wait for the Device and adaptor to dry completely before plugging them in again. Do not attempt to dry your Device or adaptor with an external heat source, such as a microwave oven or hair dryer.

Only handle your Device with clean hands. Clean the Device by unplugging it and using a soft cloth that is slightly damp with water and a touch of rubbing alcohol; be careful not to wipe it with anything abrasive or use any harsh chemicals. After cleaning, wait for the Device to dry completely before plugging it in again.

Don’t expose your Device to steam, extreme heat or cold. For example, do not install your Device near any heat sources such as space heaters, heater vents, radiators, stoves or other things that produce heat. Use your Device in a location where temperatures remain within the Device’s operating temperature range set forth in this guide. Your Device may get warm during normal use. To reduce the effect of the warming, place your Device on a heat resistant surface.

Use of third-party accessories may impact your Device’s performance. In limited circumstances, use of third-party accessories may void your Device’s limited warranty. Read all safety instructions for any accessories before using with your Device.

Do not force the power adaptor into a power outlet. If the power adaptor or cable appears damaged, discontinue use immediately. Do not plug multiple electric devices into the same wall socket. Overloading a socket can cause it to overheat and may result in a fire.

Other Safety Considerations

  • Install your power adaptor into an easily accessible socket-outlet located near the equipment that will be plugged into, or powered by, the adaptor.

Using Your Device Around Other Electronic Devices

Your Device generates, uses and can radiate radio frequency (RF) energy and, if not used in accordance with its instructions, may cause interference to radio communications and electronic equipment. External RF signals may affect improperly installed or inadequately shielded electronic operating systems, entertainment systems and personal medical devices. While most modern electronic equipment is shielded from external RF signals, if in doubt, check with the manufacturer. For personal medical devices (such as pacemakers and hearing aids), consult your physician or the manufacturer to determine whether they are adequately shielded from external RF signals.

Safety and Compliance Information for Kindle (10th Generation)

IMPORTANT PRODUCT INFORMATION

Use Responsibly. Read all instructions and safety information before use.

FAILURE TO FOLLOW THESE SAFETY INSTRUCTIONS COULD RESULT IN FIRE, ELECTRIC SHOCK, OR OTHER INJURY OR DAMAGE

Maintaining Your Kindle Device

Do not use your Kindle device or its accessories in rain, or near sinks or other wet locations. Take care not to spill any food or liquid on your Kindle. If your device does get wet, unplug all cables, turn off the wireless (tap the Settings icon and enable Airplane Mode) and let the screen revert to the screensaver. Wait for the device to dry completely before pressing the Power button to wake it again. Do not attempt to dry your Kindle with an external heat source, such as a microwave oven or hair dryer. Clean the screen with a soft cloth; be careful not to wipe it with anything abrasive. When carrying your Kindle in a bag or briefcase, keep a cover on to avoid scratches.

General Care

Clean the screen with a soft cloth; be careful not to wipe it with anything abrasive. When carrying your Kindle in a bag or briefcase, keep a cover on to avoid scratches.

Don’t expose your Kindle to extreme heat or cold. For example, don’t leave it in the trunk of your car in sub-zero or high-heat conditions.

Use of third-party accessories may impact your Kindle device’s performance. In limited circumstances, use of third-party accessories may void your Kindle device’s limited warranty. Read all safety instructions for any accessories before using them with your Kindle.

Servicing Your Device

If your device needs service, please contact Amazon Customer Support. Contact details can be found at 0.

Faulty service may void the warranty.

AVOID PROLONGED LISTENING AT HIGH VOLUME. Prolonged listening of the player at high volume can damage the ear of the user. To prevent possible hearing damage, users should not listen at high volume levels for long periods.

Battery Safety

HANDLE WITH CARE. Your Kindle contains a rechargeable lithium-ion polymer battery and should be replaced only by an authorized service provider. Do not disassemble, open, crush, bend, deform, puncture, shred or attempt to access the battery in your Kindle. Do not modify or remanufacture the battery, attempt to insert foreign objects into the battery, or immerse or expose it to water or other liquids, expose to fire, explosion, or other hazard. Only use the battery for the system for which it is specified. Use of an unqualified battery or charger may present a risk of fire, explosion, leakage, or other hazard. Do not short circuit a battery or allow metallic conductive objects to contact battery terminals. Avoid dropping the device or battery. If the device or battery is dropped, especially on a hard surface, and the user suspects damage, do not attempt repair. Contact Amazon Customer Service for assistance.

Keep the device and its power adapter in a well-ventilated area and away from heat sources, especially when in use or charging. For more information about batteries, go to 0. Your Kindle should only be charged using the USB cable included with the device, or another USB cable approved for use with your Kindle, with a compatible USB charger or USB port that meets the following requirements: USB-IF Battery Charging Spec, Rev 1.2. Do not charge your Kindle near water or in extremely humid conditions.

Other Safety Considerations

  • Engaging in repetitive motions such as pressing keys or playing some games may cause you to experience occasional discomfort in your hands, arms, shoulders, neck, or other parts of your body. Discontinue use of your device and consult a physician if you experience headaches, blackouts, seizures, convulsion, eye or muscle twitching, loss of awareness, involuntary movement, disorientation, or other discomfort. To reduce risk of these symptoms, avoid prolonged use, hold your device some distance from your eyes, use your device in a well-lit room, and take frequent breaks.
  • Small parts contained in your device and its accessories may present a choking hazard to small children.
  • Using your Kindle while operating a vehicle is not recommended and may be prohibited or restricted in some areas. If you are in an area where using your Kindle while operating a vehicle is allowed, always give full attention to the operation of the vehicle and stop using your Kindle if it becomes a distraction.
  • Personal medical devices, such as pacemakers, may be sensitive to magnetic and electromagnetic fields. Since Kindle and certain Kindle accessories contain magnets and emit electromagnetic fields, they should be kept at least 6 inches (approximately 15 cm) from any personal medical device. If any interference is observed, consult with your physician before resuming use. Items with magnetically-stored data, such as credit cards and hard drives, may also be sensitive to magnetic or electromagnetic fields, and should not be placed near these Kindle products.

Wireless Safety and Compliance

Turn your wireless connection off in areas where wireless use is forbidden or when it may cause interference or danger. Some specific situations are described below. In general, you should not use your Kindle with your wireless connection turned on any place you are not allowed to use a cellular device.

Turn Off Wireless When Flying

To prevent possible interference with aircraft systems, the U.S. Federal Aviation Administration regulations and many other aviation agencies require you to have permission from a crew member to use your device’s wireless service.

Using Your Kindle Around Other Electronic Devices

Kindle generates, uses, and can radiate radio frequency (RF) energy and, if not used in accordance with its instructions, may cause interference to radio communications and electronic equipment. External RF signals may affect improperly installed or inadequately shielded electronic operating systems, entertainment systems, and personal medical devices. While most modern electronic equipment is shielded from external RF signals, if in doubt, check with the manufacturer. For personal medical devices (such as pacemakers and hearing aids), consult with your physician or the manufacturer to determine if they are adequately shielded from external RF signals.

Steps to Minimize Interference

If your Kindle does cause interference to radio or television reception (which you can determine by turning your Kindle off and on), you can try to correct the interference by one or more of the following measures: reorient or relocate the receiving antenna for the radio or television; increase the separation between the radio or television and your Kindle; connect equipment and receivers to different outlets; or consult the radio or television manufacturer or an experienced radio/TV technician for help.

Watch for Signs

There are some places where RF signals could constitute a hazard, such as health care facilities, and construction sites. If you are not sure, look around for signs indicating that two-way radios or cell phones should be turned off.

In addition, turn off and do not charge your device if you are in any area with a potentially explosive atmosphere, as sparks in such areas could cause an explosion or fire. Such areas are usually, but not always, clearly identified, and include areas where you would normally be advised to turn off your vehicle engine.

Réduire l’exposition aux rayonnements des équipements radioélectriques

Pour réduire l’exposition aux rayonnements de l’appareil, utilisez-le dans un endroit où vous avez de bonnes conditions de réception. Only use the battery for the system for which it is specified. Use of an unqualified battery or charger may present a risk of fire, explosion, leakage or other hazard. Do not short-circuit a battery or allow metallic conductive objects to come into contact with battery terminals. Avoid dropping the device or battery. If the device or battery is dropped, especially on a hard surface, and the user suspects damage, do not attempt repair. Contact Amazon Customer Service for assistance. Keep the device and its power adapter (sold separately) in a well-ventilated area and away from heat sources, especially when in use or charging. For more information about batteries, go to 0. Your device uses the USB cable provided and a USB charger or USB port that is compliant with safety standards, such as IEC 62368. Do not charge your device near water or in extremely humid conditions. For models that support use of a wireless charger, ensure there are no metallic or magnetic foreign objects on the charger (such as coins, jewelry, metallic cases, keys, bank cards or other items) when charging. In limited circumstances, use of third-party accessories may void your device’s limited warranty. Read all safety instructions for any accessories before using with your device. It is normal for your device and charger to become warm while charging. If your device becomes uncomfortably hot while charging, remove the device from the charger and wait until it cools down before resuming charging. If your device feels too warm to use after charging, wait until it cools down.

Other Safety Considerations

  • Engaging in repetitive motions such as pressing keys or playing some games may cause you to experience occasional discomfort in your hands, arms, shoulders, neck, or other parts of your body. Discontinue use of your device and consult a physician if you experience headaches, blackouts, seizures, convulsion, eye or muscle twitching, loss of awareness, involuntary movement, disorientation, or other discomfort. To reduce risk of these symptoms, avoid prolonged use, hold your device some distance from your eyes, use your device in a well-lit room, and take frequent breaks.
  • Small parts contained in your device and its accessories may present a choking hazard to small children.
  • Using your Kindle while operating a vehicle is not recommended and may be prohibited or restricted in some areas. If you are in an area where using your Kindle while operating a vehicle is allowed, always give full attention to the operation of the vehicle and stop using your Kindle if it becomes a distraction.
  • USE CAUTION AROUND PACEMAKERS AND SIMILAR MEDICAL DEVICES. Personal medical devices, such as pacemakers, may be sensitive to magnetic and electromagnetic fields. Since the Device and certain accessories contain magnets and emit electromagnetic fields, they should be kept at least 15 cm (approximately 6 inches) from any personal medical device. If any interference is observed, consult with your doctor before resuming use. Items with magnetically-stored data, such as credit cards and hard drives, may also be sensitive to magnetic or electromagnetic fields and should not be placed near these products.

Wireless Safety and Compliance

Turn your wireless connection off in areas where wireless use is forbidden or when it may cause interference or danger. Some specific situations are described below. In general, you should not use your Kindle with your wireless connection turned on any place you are not allowed to use a cellular device.

Turn Off Wireless When Flying

To prevent possible interference with aircraft systems, the U.S. Federal Aviation Administration regulations and many other aviation agencies require you to have permission from a crew member to use your device’s wireless service.

Using Your Kindle Around Other Electronic Devices

Kindle generates, uses, and can radiate radio frequency (RF) energy and, if not used in accordance with its instructions, may cause interference to radio communications and electronic equipment. External RF signals may affect improperly installed or inadequately shielded electronic operating systems, entertainment systems, and personal medical devices. While most modern electronic equipment is shielded from external RF signals, if in doubt, check with the manufacturer. For personal medical devices (such as pacemakers and hearing aids), consult with your physician or the manufacturer to determine if they are adequately shielded from external RF signals.

Steps to Minimize Interference

If your Kindle does cause interference to radio or television reception (which you can determine by turning your Kindle off and on), you can try to correct the interference by one or more of the following measures: reorient or relocate the receiving antenna for the radio or television; increase the separation between the radio or television and your Kindle; connect equipment and receivers to different outlets; or consult the radio or television manufacturer or an experienced radio/TV technician for help.

Watch for Signs

There are some places where RF signals could constitute a hazard, such as health care facilities, and construction sites. If you are not sure, look around for signs indicating that two-way radios or cell phones should be turned off. In addition, turn off and do not charge your device if you are in any area with a potentially explosive atmosphere, as sparks in such areas could cause an explosion or fire. Such areas are usually, but not always, clearly identified, and include areas where you would normally be advised to turn off your vehicle engine. Instructions for product handling during and after usage

Dos:

  4. Always call Authorised E-Waste Recyclers to dispose of products that have reached their end of life.
  5. Always drop your used electronic products, batteries or any accessories at your nearest Authorized E-Waste Collection Point when they have reached their end of life.

Don’ts:

    1. Do not dismantle your electronic products on your own.
  2. Do not throw electronics into bins featuring a “Do not dispose” sign.
  3. Do not give e-waste to informal and unorganized sectors such as local scrap dealers/ragpickers.
  4. Do not throw your product into bins along with municipal waste that ultimately reaches landfills.

Safety and Compliance Information for Kindle Colorsoft Signature Edition

IMPORTANT PRODUCT INFORMATIONSAFETY INFORMATION

READ ALL SAFETY INFORMATION BEFORE USING THE DEVICE. FAILURE TO FOLLOW THESE SAFETY INSTRUCTIONS COULD RESULT IN FIRE, ELECTRIC SHOCK, OR OTHER INJURY OR DAMAGE.

BATTERY SAFETY

HANDLE WITH CARE. Your device contains a rechargeable lithium‐ion battery and should be replaced only by a qualified service provider. Do not disassemble, remove or attempt to modify or access the battery in your device. Avoid dropping the device or battery. If the device or battery is dropped, especially on a hard surface,and the user suspects damage, do not attempt repair. Contact Amazon Customer Service for assistance.

Keep the device and its power adaptor (sold separately) in a well‐ventilated area and away from heat sources, especially when in use or charging. For more information about batteries, go to 0 Your device uses the USB cable provided and a USB charger or USB port that is compliant with safety standards, such as IEC 62368. Do not charge your device near water or in extremely humid conditions.

For models that support use of a wireless charger, ensure there are no metallic or magnetic foreign objects on the charger (such as coins, jewellery, metallic cases, keys, bank cards or other items) when charging. In limited circumstances, use of third‐party accessories may void your device’s limited warranty. Read all safety instructions for any accessories before using with your device.

CHOKING HAZARD – SMALL PARTS.

Small parts contained in your device and its accessories may present a choking hazard to small children.

Protection Against Water

Your device has been tested to withstand brief accidental immersion in water under certain conditions. However,your device is not intended for underwater use and may experience temporary effects from exposure to water. If your device is immersed in water, follow these instructions:

  • Quickly retrieve your device. If your device has a cover, remove the cover.
  • If your device was immersed in fresh water, unplug all cables and drain your device by letting it stand upright with the USB port facing down.
  • If your device was immersed in other types of water, unplug all cables, drain your device by letting it stand upright with the USB port facing down, rinse your device off using fresh water (such as cold tap water) with the USB port facing the water source, and drain your device again.
  • Let the device dry fully in a well‐ventilated location before plugging the USB cable into your device.
  • Do not attempt to dry your device with an external heat source (such as a microwave oven or hairdryer).
  • Do not insert a foreign object into the USB port (such as a cotton bud or paper towel).

To reduce the risk of damage to your device, follow these instructions:

  • Do not intentionally immerse your device in water or expose it to seawater, salt water, chlorinated water or other liquids (such as beverages).
  • Do not spill any food, oil, lotion or abrasive substances on your device.
  • Do not expose your device to pressurised water, high‐velocity water or extremely humid conditions (such as a steam room).

If your device is dropped or otherwise damaged, the waterproofing of the device may be compromised. For more information concerning care instructions and the waterproofing of your device, please see 0.

General Care

Clean the screen with a soft cloth; be careful not to wipe it with anything abrasive. When carrying your Kindle in a bag or briefcase, keep a cover on to avoid scratches.

Don’t expose your Kindle to extreme heat or cold. For example, don’t leave it in the trunk of your car in sub‐zero or high‐heat conditions.

Use of third‐party accessories may impact your Kindle device’s performance. In limited circumstances, use of third‐party accessories may void your Kindle device’s limited warranty. Read all safety instructions for any accessories before using them with your Kindle.

Other Safety Considerations

Engaging in repetitive motions such as pressing keys or playing some games may cause you to experience occasionaldiscomfort in your hands, arms, shoulders, neck or other parts of your body. Discontinue use of your device and consult a physician if you experience headaches, blackouts, seizures, convulsion, eye or muscle twitching, loss of awareness, involuntary movement, disorientation or other discomfort. To reduce risk of these symptoms, avoid prolonged use, hold your device some distance from your eyes, use your device in a well‐lit room, and take frequent breaks.

Using your Kindle while operating a vehicle is not recommended and may be prohibited or restricted in some areas. If you are in an area where using your Kindle while operating a vehicle is allowed, always give full attention to the operation of the vehicle and stop using your Kindle if it becomes a distraction.

USE CAUTION AROUND PACEMAKERS AND SIMILAR MEDICAL DEVICES. Personal medical devices, such as pacemakers, may be sensitive to magnetic and electromagnetic fields. Since the Device and certain accessories contain magnets and emit electromagnetic fields, they should be kept at least 15 cm (approximately 6 inches) from any personal medical device. If any interference is observed, consult with your doctor before resuminguse. Items with magnetically‐stored data, such as credit cards and hard drives, may also be sensitive to magnetic or electromagnetic fields and should not be placed near these products.

Wireless Safety and Compliance

Turn your wireless connection off in areas where wireless use is forbidden or when it may cause interference or danger. Some specific situations are described below. In general, you should not use your Kindle with your wireless connection turned on any place you are not allowed to use a cellular device.

Turn Off Wireless When Flying

To prevent possible interference with aircraft systems, the US Federal Aviation Administration regulations and many other aviation agencies require you to have permission from a crew member to use your device’s wireless service.

Using Your Kindle Around Other Electronic Devices

Kindle generates, uses and can radiate radio frequency (RF) energy and, if not used in accordance with its instructions, may cause interference to radio communications and electronic equipment. External RF signals may affect improperly installed or inadequately shielded electronic operating systems, entertainment systems and personal medical devices. While most modern electronic equipment is shielded from external RF signals,if in doubt, check with the manufacturer. For personal medical devices (such as pacemakers and hearing aids), consult with your physician or the manufacturer to determine if they are adequately shielded from external RF signals.

Steps to Minimize Interference

If your Kindle does cause interference to radio or television reception (which you can determine by turning your Kindle off and on), you can try to correct the interference by one or more of the following measures: reorient or relocate the receiving antenna for the radio or television; increase the separation between the radio or television and your Kindle; connect equipment and receivers to different sockets; or consult the radio or television manufacturer or an experienced radio/TV technician for help.

Watch for Signs

There are some places where RF signals could constitute a hazard, such as healthcare facilities and construction sites. If you are not sure, look around for signs indicating that two‐way radios or cell phones should be turned off.

In addition, turn off and do not charge your device if you are in any area with a potentially explosive atmosphere, assparks in such areas could cause an explosion or fire. Such areas are usually, but not always, clearly identified, and include areas where you would normally be advised to turn off your vehicle engine.

AVOID PROLONGED LISTENING AT HIGH VOLUME. Prolonged listening of the player at high volume can damage the ear of the user. To prevent possible hearing damage, users should not listen at high volume levels for long periods.

Model Number: SA59CP

Electrical Rating: 5 V ⎓ 1.5 A

Operating Temperature Range: 0°C to 35°C (32°F to 95°F)

FOR CUSTOMERS IN USAFCC COMPLIANCE

This device and its related accessories like the adapter (the “Products”) comply with part 15 of the FCC Rules. Operation of each Product is subject to the following two conditions: (1) each Product may not cause harmful interference,and (2) each Product must accept any interference received, including interference that may cause undesired operation.

Note: The Products have been tested and found to comply with the limits for a Class B digital device or external switching power supply, pursuant to part 15 of the FCC Rules. These limits are designed to provide reasonable protection against harmful interference in a residential installation. The Products generate, use and can radiate radio frequency energy and, if not installed and used in accordance with the instructions, may cause harmful interference to radio communications. However, there is no guarantee that interference will not occur in a particular installation. If a Product does cause harmful interference to radio or television reception, which can be determined by turning the Product off and on, the user is encouraged to try to correct the interference by one or more of the following measures:

  • Reorient or relocate the receiving antenna.
  • Increase the separation between the equipment and receiver.
  • Connect the equipment to a socket on a circuit different from that to which the receiver is connected.
  • Consult the dealer or an experienced radio/TV technician for help.

Safety and Compliance Information for Kindle Colorsoft

IMPORTANT PRODUCT INFORMATIONSAFETY INFORMATION

READ ALL SAFETY INFORMATION BEFORE USING THE DEVICE. FAILURE TO FOLLOW THESE SAFETY INSTRUCTIONS COULD RESULT IN FIRE, ELECTRIC SHOCK, OR OTHER INJURY OR DAMAGE.

BATTERY SAFETY

HANDLE WITH CARE. Your device contains a rechargeable lithium‐ion battery and should be replaced only by a qualified service provider. Do not disassemble, remove or attempt to modify or access the battery in your device. Avoid dropping the device or battery. If the device or battery is dropped, especially on a hard surface,and the user suspects damage, do not attempt repair. Contact Amazon Customer Service for assistance.

Keep the device and its power adaptor (sold separately) in a well‐ventilated area and away from heat sources, especially when in use or charging. For more information about batteries, go to 0 Your device uses the USB cable provided and a USB charger or USB port that is compliant with safety standards, such as IEC 62368. Do not charge your device near water or in extremely humid conditions.

CHOKING HAZARD – SMALL PARTS.

Small parts contained in your device and its accessories may present a choking hazard to small children.

Protection Against Water

Your device has been tested to withstand brief accidental immersion in water under certain conditions. However,your device is not intended for underwater use and may experience temporary effects from exposure to water. If your device is immersed in water, follow these instructions:

  • Quickly retrieve your device. If your device has a cover, remove the cover.
  • If your device was immersed in fresh water, unplug all cables and drain your device by letting it stand upright with the USB port facing down.
  • If your device was immersed in other types of water, unplug all cables, drain your device by letting it stand upright with the USB port facing down, rinse your device off using fresh water (such as cold tap water) with the USB port facing the water source, and drain your device again.
  • Let the device dry fully in a well‐ventilated location before plugging the USB cable into your device.
  • Do not attempt to dry your device with an external heat source (such as a microwave oven or hairdryer).
  • Do not insert a foreign object into the USB port (such as a cotton bud or paper towel).

To reduce the risk of damage to your device, follow these instructions:

  • Do not intentionally immerse your device in water or expose it to seawater, salt water, chlorinated water or other liquids (such as beverages).
  • Do not spill any food, oil, lotion or abrasive substances on your device.
  • Do not expose your device to pressurised water, high‐velocity water or extremely humid conditions (such as a steam room).

If your device is dropped or otherwise damaged, the waterproofing of the device may be compromised. For more information concerning care instructions and the waterproofing of your device, please see 0.

General Care

Clean the screen with a soft cloth; be careful not to wipe it with anything abrasive. When carrying your Kindle in a bag or briefcase, keep a cover on to avoid scratches.

Don’t expose your Kindle to extreme heat or cold. For example, don’t leave it in the trunk of your car in sub‐zero or high‐heat conditions.

Use of third‐party accessories may impact your Kindle device’s performance. In limited circumstances, use of third‐party accessories may void your Kindle device’s limited warranty. Read all safety instructions for any accessories before using them with your Kindle.

Other Safety Considerations

Engaging in repetitive motions such as pressing keys or playing some games may cause you to experience occasionaldiscomfort in your hands, arms, shoulders, neck or other parts of your body. Discontinue use of your device and consult a physician if you experience headaches, blackouts, seizures, convulsion, eye or muscle twitching, loss of awareness, involuntary movement, disorientation or other discomfort. To reduce risk of these symptoms, avoid prolonged use, hold your device some distance from your eyes, use your device in a well‐lit room, and take frequent breaks.

Using your Kindle while operating a vehicle is not recommended and may be prohibited or restricted in some areas. If you are in an area where using your Kindle while operating a vehicle is allowed, always give full attention to the operation of the vehicle and stop using your Kindle if it becomes a distraction.

USE CAUTION AROUND PACEMAKERS AND SIMILAR MEDICAL DEVICES. Personal medical devices, such as pacemakers, may be sensitive to magnetic and electromagnetic fields. Since the Device and certain accessories contain magnets and emit electromagnetic fields, they should be kept at least 15 cm (approximately 6 inches) from any personal medical device. If any interference is observed, consult with your doctor before resuminguse. Items with magnetically‐stored data, such as credit cards and hard drives, may also be sensitive to magnetic or electromagnetic fields and should not be placed near these products.

Wireless Safety and Compliance

Turn your wireless connection off in areas where wireless use is forbidden or when it may cause interference or danger. Some specific situations are described below. In general, you should not use your Kindle with your wireless connection turned on any place you are not allowed to use a cellular device.

Turn Off Wireless When Flying

To prevent possible interference with aircraft systems, the US Federal Aviation Administration regulations and many other aviation agencies require you to have permission from a crew member to use your device’s wireless service.

Safety and Compliance Information for Kindle Colorsoft Kids

IMPORTANT PRODUCT INFORMATIONSAFETY INFORMATION

READ ALL SAFETY INFORMATION BEFORE USING THE DEVICE. FAILURE TO FOLLOW THESE SAFETY INSTRUCTIONS COULD RESULT IN FIRE, ELECTRIC SHOCK, OR OTHER INJURY OR DAMAGE.

CAUTION– ELECTRICALLY OPERATED PRODUCT: Not recommended for children under 7 years of age.

As with all electric products, precautions should be observed during handling and use to prevent electric shock.

The device and its included accessories are not toys. Children should be supervised to ensure proper use. Parents should also routinely check to confirm that the device shows no signs of damage or breakage and is in safe condition for use by children. The device is not suitable for children under age 7.

ALWAYSINSPECT FOR BREAKAGE AND USE WITH CHILD‐FRIENDLY COVER. If the device is dropped or impacted, parents should remove the child‐friendly cover and inspect the device and cover for breaks, chips or cracks before putting the cover back on the device and permitting a child to resume use.

BATTERY SAFETY

HANDLE WITH CARE. Your device contains a rechargeable lithium‐ion battery and should be replaced only by a qualified service provider. Do not disassemble, remove or attempt to modify or access the battery in your device. Avoid dropping the device or battery. If the device or battery is dropped, especially on a hard surface,and the user suspects damage, do not attempt repair. Contact Amazon Customer Service for assistance.

Keep the device and its power adaptor (sold separately) in a well‐ventilated area and away from heat sources, especially when in use or charging. For more information about batteries, go to 0 Your device uses the USB cable provided and a USB charger or USB port that is compliant with safety standards, such as IEC 62368. Do not charge your device near water or in extremely humid conditions.

CHOKING HAZARD – SMALL PARTS.

Small parts contained in your device and its accessories may present a choking hazard to small children.

Protection Against Water

Your device has been tested to withstand brief accidental immersion in water under certain conditions. However,your device is not intended for underwater use and may experience temporary effects from exposure to water. If your device is immersed in water, follow these instructions:

  • Quickly retrieve your device. If your device has a cover, remove the cover.
  • If your device was immersed in fresh water, unplug all cables and drain your device by letting it stand upright with the USB port facing down.
  • If your device was immersed in other types of water, unplug all cables, drain your device by letting it stand upright with the USB port facing down, rinse your device off using fresh water (such as cold tap water) with the USB port facing the water source, and drain your device again.
  • Let the device dry fully in a well‐ventilated location before plugging the USB cable into your device.
  • Do not attempt to dry your device with an external heat source (such as a microwave oven or hairdryer).
  • Do not insert a foreign object into the USB port (such as a cotton bud or paper towel).

To reduce the risk of damage to your device, follow these instructions:

  • Do not intentionally immerse your device in water or expose it to seawater, salt water, chlorinated water or other liquids (such as beverages).
  • Do not spill any food, oil, lotion or abrasive substances on your device.
  • Do not expose your device to pressurised water, high‐velocity water or extremely humid conditions (such as a steam room).

If your device is dropped or otherwise damaged, the waterproofing of the device may be compromised. For more information concerning care instructions and the waterproofing of your device, please see 0.

General Care

Clean the screen with a soft cloth; be careful not to wipe it with anything abrasive. When carrying your Kindle in a bag or briefcase, keep a cover on to avoid scratches.

Don’t expose your Kindle to extreme heat or cold. For example, don’t leave it in the trunk of your car in sub‐zero or high‐heat conditions.

Use of third‐party accessories may impact your Kindle device’s performance. In limited circumstances, use of third‐party accessories may void your Kindle device’s limited warranty. Read all safety instructions for any accessories before using them with your Kindle.

Other Safety Considerations

Engaging in repetitive motions such as pressing keys or playing some games may cause you to experience occasionaldiscomfort in your hands, arms, shoulders, neck or other parts of your body. Discontinue use of your device and consult a physician if you experience headaches, blackouts, seizures, convulsion, eye or muscle twitching, loss of awareness, involuntary movement, disorientation or other discomfort. To reduce risk of these symptoms, avoid prolonged use, hold your device some distance from your eyes, use your device in a well‐lit room, and take frequent breaks.

Using your Kindle while operating a vehicle is not recommended and may be prohibited or restricted in some areas. If you are in an area where using your Kindle while operating a vehicle is allowed, always give full attention to the operation of the vehicle and stop using your Kindle if it becomes a distraction.

USE CAUTION AROUND PACEMAKERS AND SIMILAR MEDICAL DEVICES. Personal medical devices, such as pacemakers, may be sensitive to magnetic and electromagnetic fields. Since the Device and certain accessories contain magnets and emit electromagnetic fields, they should be kept at least 15 cm (approximately 6 inches) from any personal medical device. If any interference is observed, consult with your doctor before resuminguse. Items with magnetically‐stored data, such as credit cards and hard drives, may also be sensitive to magnetic or electromagnetic fields and should not be placed near these products.

Wireless Safety and Compliance

Turn your wireless connection off in areas where wireless use is forbidden or when it may cause interference or danger. Some specific situations are described below. In general, you should not use your Kindle with your wireless connection turned on any place you are not allowed to use a cellular device.

Turn Off Wireless When Flying

To prevent possible interference with aircraft systems, the US Federal Aviation Administration regulations and many other aviation agencies require you to have permission from a crew member to use your device’s wireless service.

Using Your Kindle Around Other Electronic Devices

Kindle generates, uses and can radiate radio frequency (RF) energy and, if not used in accordance with its instructions, may cause interference to radio communications and electronic equipment. External RF signals may affect improperly installed or inadequately shielded electronic operating systems, entertainment systems and personal medical devices. While most modern electronic equipment is shielded from external RF signals,if in doubt, check with the manufacturer. For personal medical devices (such as pacemakers and hearing aids), consult with your physician or the manufacturer to determine if they are adequately shielded from external RF signals.

Steps to Minimize Interference

If your Kindle does cause interference to radio or television reception (which you can determine by turning your Kindle off and on), you can try to correct the interference by one or more of the following measures: reorient or relocate the receiving antenna for the radio or television; increase the separation between the radio or television and your Kindle; connect equipment and receivers to different sockets; or consult the radio or television manufacturer or an experienced radio/TV technician for help.

Watch for Signs

There are some places where RF signals could constitute a hazard, such as healthcare facilities and construction sites. If you are not sure, look around for signs indicating that two‐way radios or cell phones should be turned off.

In addition, turn off and do not charge your device if you are in any area with a potentially explosive atmosphere, assparks in such areas could cause an explosion or fire. Such areas are usually, but not always, clearly identified, and include areas where you would normally be advised to turn off your vehicle engine.

AVOID PROLONGED LISTENING AT HIGH VOLUME. Prolonged listening of the player at high volume can damage the ear of the user. To prevent possible hearing damage, users should not listen at high volume levels for long periods.

This device and its related accessories like the adapter (the “Products”) comply with part 15 of the FCC Rules. Operation of each Product is subject to the following two conditions: (1) each Product may not cause harmful interference,and (2) each Product must accept any interference received, including interference that may cause undesired operation.

Note: The Products have been tested and found to comply with the limits for a Class B digital device or external switching power supply, pursuant to part 15 of the FCC Rules. These limits are designed to provide reasonable protection against harmful interference in a residential installation. The Products generate, use and can radiate radio frequency energy and, if not installed and used in accordance with the instructions, may cause harmful interference to radio communications. However, there is no guarantee that interference will not occur in a particular installation. If a Product does cause harmful interference to radio or television reception, which can be determined by turning the Product off and on, the user is encouraged to try to correct the interference by one or more of the following measures:

  • Reorient or relocate the receiving antenna.
  • Increase the separation between the equipment and receiver.
  • Connect the equipment to a socket on a circuit different from that to which the receiver is connected.
  • Consult the dealer or an experienced radio/TV technician for help.

Send Kindle Books to a Group

Use the provided spreadsheet after checkout, with tools like mail merge, to send book redemption links to multiple recipients at once.

  1. Go to Your Digital Orders.
  2. Locate your order and select Manage eBooks.
  3. Select Download spreadsheet. The spreadsheet is a comma separated value (CSV) file containing redemption links that have not been redeemed.
  4. Open the file and fill in your recipients’ names and email addresses.
  5. Download an email template and open it.
  6. Customize the message and fill in the highlighted portion.
  7. Use a tool like mail merge to combine the template and spreadsheet and send out emails.

Note:

  • If you don’t live in the same country as your recipient, the book won’t be available for your recipient. The recipient is unable to redeem the book and you may distribute that copy to another recipient or request a refund on the unredeemed copy.

Send Kindle Books to Individual Recipients

When buying books for others using the “Buy for others” option, you can send books to recipients by entering their email during your checkout or do it from Your Orders.

  1. Go to Your Orders.
  2. Locate your order and select Manage eBooks.
  3. Select Copy link with instructions next to the link you’d like to send.
  4. Open your email program and paste the link and instructions into the body of a new email.
  5. Enter the recipient’s email address and the email subject, and send the email.

Note:

  • If you don’t live in the same country as your recipient, the book won’t be available for your recipient. The recipient is unable to redeem the book and you may distribute that copy to another recipient or request a refund on the unredeemed copy.

Send Kindle books to single or multiple recipients

You can send Kindle books purchased with your business account to 1 recipient at a time, or you can send books to multiple recipients at once.

To send Kindle books:

  1. Go to Your Digital Orders.
  2. Find your order and select Manage eBooks.
  3. To send a Kindle book to 1 participant: 1. Select Copy link with instructions next to the link that you’d like to send. It copies the redemption link, along with instructions on how to redeem the book. 2. Paste the link and instructions into an email to the recipient, and send.
  4. To send Kindle books to multiple recipients at once: 1. Select Download spreadsheet. 2. Open the downloaded spreadsheet and fill in the names and email addresses of your recipients. 3. Download and open this email template. 4. Customize the message and fill in the highlighted portion. 5. Use a tool like mail merge to combine the template and spreadsheet and send out emails. Note: The spreadsheet is a comma-separated value (CSV) file that contains redemption links that no-one has redeemed. When you download the file, it captures the status of each redemption link. You can open the file in a spreadsheet program such as Microsoft Excel.

Track the redemption status of an audiobook

You can purchase Audible audiobooks in bulk with an Amazon Business account, distribute them using redemption codes, and track redemption status.

To manage bulk orders, go to the order details in your Amazon Business account.

To track an audiobook’s redemption status:

  1. Sign in to your Amazon Business account and go to Your Orders.
  2. Go to order details and select Manage audiobook.
  3. Select Redeemed or Not redeemed to filter by status.
  4. To re-share redemption codes and instructions, select Copy instructions, and then paste the pre-written message in an email.

Note: Only recipients in the US can use redemption codes. You cannot resell redemption codes.

For Amazon Business customers: If you have an Amazon Business account and need assistance with your bulk audiobook order, .

For individual Audible users: For help with your Audible account, your purchases and listening to audiobooks, or to contact Audible Customer Care, visit the Audible Help Center .

Transfer Your Kindle Content to Another Country or Region

If your country or region has changed, you can transfer Kindle content you’ve purchased to match your new local Amazon marketplace. Kindle Unlimited subscriptions can’t be transferred.

Transferring your Amazon account to another country or region transfers your digital content to the new account. It also allows you to purchase content from the Kindle Store in your local currency.

  1. Go to Manage Your Content and Devices and click Preferences.
  2. Click Change under Country Settings.
  3. Select or enter your address, and click Update.
  4. Follow the on-screen prompts to learn more about what will happen if you transfer your Amazon account.
  5. When you’re ready, select the option to transfer your account to complete the process.

Note:

Kindle Unlimited subscriptions can’t be transferred to a new country or region. If your country changes, your subscription is cancelled and your titles borrowed through Kindle Unlimited are automatically returned.

After your account is transferred, you can check if Kindle Unlimited is available in your new country or region by visiting Kindle Unlimited.

Other subscription services, such as Subscribe with Amazon, will also be cancelled.

Use Alexa to Read Your Kindle Books

Turn on Kindle Assistive Reader to read books with Alexa and control Alexa’s reading with your voice.

Note: Kindle Assistive Reader isn’t available on Alexa+.

Kindle Assistive Reader is designed to support customers with disabilities to read Kindle books. Kindle Assistive Reader is available on all Alexa built-in devices and uses Alexa’s underlying Text-to-Speech capabilities.

To turn on Kindle Assistive Reader, simply ask Alexa to read a book or turn it on in your Alexa app accessibility settings. For more information, go to Turn On Kindle Assistive Reader on Echo Devices with a Screen.

While Alexa reads, you can ask Alexa to do things like:

  • Read a specific Kindle book title

  • Share more information about the book being read

  • Pause, stop, or restart reading the book

  • Navigate through your book by chapters, or by time intervals

  • Slow down or speed up Alexa’s reading speed

  • Set or cancel a timer for Alexa to stop reading

  • Which Books Can Alexa Read with Kindle Assistive Reader?

  • Find Kindle Books in the Alexa App

View Your Notebook in Kindle for Web

Manage your notes, highlights, and bookmarks from a single list.

  1. To reveal the menu bar, move your mouse pointer to the top of the app.
  2. Click the Notebook icon. All highlights, notes, and bookmarks are displayed.
  3. To delete an item, choose the Trash icon.
  4. To move a specific page, click the item in the list.
  5. To close the notebook, click the X.

What is Kindle for Web?

Kindle for Web lets you read your Kindle books on your mobile or desktop browser. If you don’t have a Kindle e-reader or the Kindle app, Kindle for Web is perfect for you.

What Is Look Inside the Book?

Look Inside the Book lets you search through and view pages from books on Amazon.

Look Inside the Book allows you to preview a limited number of pages of books. This means you can sample books before you purchase. Samples for print books allow you to search for words in the book to confirm that the book is just what they’re looking for

Our agreements with publishers and copyright holders currently limit how much of the book is available for preview. Pages, or parts of pages, will be omitted due to copyright restrictions.

The images are optimized for display on a computer monitor; printed pages usually have a much higher resolution and better quality.

If samples of the requested format of the book are not available, we will try and show the samples from related editions. We will indicate if the sample comes from the paperback, hardcover, or Kindle edition of the book.

Some features and pages are only available if you’re logged into your Amazon account and have made a prior purchase. If you choose not to log in, or haven’t made a purchase, we provide only a limited set of preview pages for the book.

What Is Search Inside the Book?

Search Inside the Book lets you view, search, and browse books on Amazon.

On Amazon, Search Inside the Book search results include matches based on every word inside a book. This works as long as there’s both a print and a Kindle edition of the book.

If you’re a publisher or author and want to include your titles in this program, contact your respective customer service for details.

Note: Because of our agreements with publishers, some features and pages are only available if you’re logged into your Amazon account and have made a prior purchase.
